## 1. Domolux Collections Hierarchy

The Domolux 3D asset library consists of three distinct engineering releases:

1. **Domolux Foundation Collection (2 assets):** The initial pilot luxury products (`domolux.executive_desk` and `domolux.luxury_spa_bathtub`).
2. **Domolux Private Collection 30 (30 assets):** The core 30-item high-luxury residential collection spanning wellness, private cinema, outdoor living, and bespoke furniture.
3. **Domolux Villa 200 Collection (200 assets):** The monumental 200-product architectural and furnishing library across 10 distinct collections (#001 to #200).
